The frozen complete-configuration pilot completed four of its six prescribed runs, then stopped when the next thermal/power preflight refused launch. Both draft-enabled configurations completed all calibration tasks twice. Each run passed fifteen of sixteen frozen task outcomes, with the same sorting-format failure. Each repeated same-artifact run preserved all prompt/output token IDs and terminal outcomes. No replacement round or retry was started.

The first baseline run met the frozen timing conditions. The two affine runs exceeded the competing-CPU criteria, and the second baseline run had a thermal/low-power exclusion. Host samples retain the background process names and CPU observations. This source is marked discarded for comparative timing: there is no paired speed aggregate, sustained-speed claim, quality noninferiority qualification or automatic promotion. Physical process peaks remained under the ten-GB watchdog for all four completed cases. The incomplete campaign receipt and refused preflight remain unchanged.

The separate context-proxies CI failed because its isolated Swift source list omitted the new PackMemoryProfile dependency. The downloaded compiler diagnostics establish the cause. Adding the actual production source to the proxy's compilation and provenance list fixes the regression. The bounded local proxy run passes without weights or an Engine. This CI harness correction does not alter numerical fixtures or performance criteria.

Local home prefixes are replaced with <HOME>. Original lengths and SHA-256 identify the unmodified files. Large normalized UTF-8 transcripts are losslessly zlib-compressed and base64 encoded. Decode with `zlib.decompress(base64.b64decode(block))` and check the normalized digest. Every encoded block is verified before writing. Frozen binaries, source archives and weights remain in bounded local staging; the receipts bind their exact bytes.
